Original source text:
The Board of Navigation Rules for Assigning Ships Separate Ice-Due Classes, 1985, (2.9.1985 No. 2575/85/307), as amended, apply to ships the keels of which have been laid or which have been at a similar stage of construction on or after 1 November 1986 but before 1 September 2003. On the owner's request, the requirements of the 2008 Ice Class Regulations may, however, be applied to the engine output of such ships.
Normalized requirement:
The Board of Navigation Rules for Assigning Ships Separate Ice-Due Classes, 1985, (2.9.1985 No. 2575/85/307), as amended, apply to ships the keels of which have been laid or which have been at a similar stage of construction on or after 1 November 1986 but before 1 September 2003. On the owner's request, the requirements of the 2008 Ice Class Regulations may, however, be applied to the engine output of such ships.
Required inputs: iceClass, mcrPower_kW

Original source text:
For category A and B ships constructed on or after 1 January 2017, the carriage of NLS identified in chapter 17, column e, as ship type 3 or identified as NLS in chapter 18 of the International Code for the Construction and Equipment of Ships Carrying Dangerous Chemicals in Bulk, in cargo tanks of type 3 ships shall be subject to the approval of the Administration. The results shall be reflected on the International Pollution Prevention Certificate for the Carriage of Noxious Liquid Substances in Bulk or Certificate of Fitness identifying the operation in polar waters.
Normalized requirement:
IF ship_category IN {'A','B'} AND construction_date >= '2017-01-01' AND carried_NLS_requires_type_3_tank = true AND cargo_tank_type = 3, THEN administration_carriage_approval_status = approved AND applicable_certificate_polar_operation_entry_present = true.

```turtle
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:carriedNoxiousLiquidSubstanceRequiresType3Tank ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The type-3-tank applicability indication is required."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:cargoTankType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A single cargo tank type is required to determine applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an applicable Category A or B ship constructed on or after 1 January 2017 carrying NLS requiring a type 3 tank in cargo tanks of type 3, Administration approval and the applicable certificate polar-operation entry are required." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:shipCategory ?category ;
                      nltl:constructionDate ?constructionDate ;
                      nltl:carriedNoxiousLiquidSubstanceRequiresType3Tank ?requiresType3 ;
                      nltl:cargoTankType ?tankType .

                FILTER (
                    ?category IN (
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryA,
                        nltl:polarShipCategoryB
                    )
                    && ?constructionDate >= "2017-01-01"^^xsd:date
                    && ?requiresType3 = true
                    && ?tankType = "3"
                )

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:administrationCarriageApprovalStatus
                          nltl:evidenceStateApproved
                }

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:administrationCarriageApprovalStatus ?status
                    FILTER (?status != nltl:evidenceStateApproved)
                }

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:applicableCertificatePolarOperationEntryPresent true
                }

                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:applicableCertificatePolarOperationEntryPresent ?entryPresent
                    FILTER (?entryPresent != true)
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Original source text:
The maximum and minimum ice class draughts at fore and aft perpendiculars shall be determined in accordance with the upper and lower ice waterlines and the draught of the ship at fore and aft perpendiculars, when ice conditions require the ship to be ice-strengthened, shall always be between the upper and lower ice waterlines.
Normalized requirement:
The maximum and minimum ice class draughts at fore and aft perpendiculars shall be determined in accordance with the upper and lower ice waterlines and the draught of the ship at fore and aft perpendiculars, when ice conditions require the ship to be ice-strengthened, shall always be between the upper and lower ice waterlines.

```turtle
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:operatingForeDraught ?ofq .
    ?ofq qudt:numericValue ?of .
    $this nltl:maximumIceClassDraughtFore ?maxfq .
    ?maxfq qudt:numericValue ?maxf .
    $this nltl:minimumIceClassDraughtFore ?minfq .
    ?minfq qudt:numericValue ?minf .
    $this nltl:operatingAftDraught ?oaq .
    ?oaq qudt:numericValue ?oa .
    $this nltl:maximumIceClassDraughtAft ?maxaq .
    ?maxaq qudt:numericValue ?maxa .
    $this nltl:minimumIceClassDraughtAft ?minaq .
    ?minaq qudt:numericValue ?mina .
    FILTER (
        ?of < ?minf ||
        ?of > ?maxf ||
        ?oa < ?mina ||
        ?oa > ?maxa
    )
}
"""
    ] .
```

Original source text:
Ship intended to operate in low air temperature means a ship which is intended to undertake voyages to or through areas where the lowest Mean Daily Low Temperature (MDLT) is below -10 deg C.
Normalized requirement:
ship_operates_in_low_air_temperature = true IF lowest_MDLT_degC < -10.
Required inputs: Lowest MDLT and declared operating-temperature class

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-013-LowMdltClassificationShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:lowestMdlt ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "A ship with a lowest MDLT below -10 deg C must be classified as operating in low air temperature." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:lowestMdlt ?quantity .
                ?quantity qudt:numericValue ?value .
                FILTER (?value < "-10"^^xsd:decimal)
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:shipOperatesInLowAirTemperature true
                }
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-012-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-012-IceWaterlineShape> ;
        sh:message "The ship must represent an intended ice-operating waterline with profile points and coordinates."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasLowerIceWaterline ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-012-IceWaterlineShape> ;
        sh:message "The ship must represent the externally determined lower ice waterline with profile points and coordinates."
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-012-IceWaterlineShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:class nltl:waterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node <urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-012-ProfilePointShape> ;
        sh:message "Each represented ice waterline must contain at least one waterline profile point."
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-012-ProfilePointShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:waterlineProfilePoint ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalPosition ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each waterline profile point must have one longitudinal position in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalCoordinate ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF012ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasIntendedIceOperatingWaterline ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF012IceWaterlineShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must represent at least one intended ice-operating waterline with profile points."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasLowerIceWaterline ;
        sh:class nltl:iceWaterline ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF012IceWaterlineShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must represent the externally determined lower ice waterline with profile points."
    ] .

gen:TRF012IceWaterlineShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasWaterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:class nltl:waterlineProfilePoint ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF012ProfilePointShape ;
        sh:message "Each represented ice waterline must have at least one waterline profile point."
    ] .

gen:TRF012ProfilePointShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:longitudinalPosition ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF012LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each waterline profile point must carry one longitudinal position in metres."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:verticalCoordinate ;
        sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF012LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each waterline profile point must carry one vertical coordinate in metres."
    ] .

gen:TRF012LengthQuantityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1
    ] .

```

```turtle
        sh:message "The ship must provide the declared longitudinal and transverse framing thickness inputs for the external interpolation calculation."
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:IMO_055_Shape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:valveExposed ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ship must provide one boolean valve exposure value for applicability determination."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:valveType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each ship must provide one valve type for applicability determination."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceAccretionProtectionPresent ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:continuouslyAccessible ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "An exposed isolating, pressure, or vacuum valve must have ice accretion protection." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:valveExposed true ;
                      nltl:valveType ?valveType .
                FILTER (?valveType IN ("isolating", "pressure", "vacuum"))
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:iceAccretionProtectionPresent true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "An exposed isolating, pressure, or vacuum valve must remain continuously accessible." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:valveExposed true ;
                      nltl:valveType ?valveType .
                FILTER (?valveType IN ("isolating", "pressure", "vacuum"))
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:continuouslyAccessible true .
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
        sh:path nltl:immersionSuitRequired ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Immersion-suit applicability must be recorded as a single boolean value."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:immersionSuitRequired ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:immersionSuitTypeClassification ;
                sh:hasValue nltl:insulatedImmersionSuit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "Where immersion suits are required, the immersion suit type must be insulated."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:immersionSuitRequired ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

CASE I2-052-I-P01
Original expected verdict: PASS
Original rationale: Complete interpolation-readiness graph contains input position, lower/upper endpoints, point coordinates/values and result fields.
Source clause: I2.13.3.1 (intermediate values by linear interpolation)
Test pattern: UNSPECIFIED
RDF path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/evaluation/BEHAVIORAL_RDF_R13/rdf/I2/I2-052/I2-052-I-P01.ttl
RDF SHA-256: ff98950136e5eb5783ac8dc79363edbbef3ab9121e2f61850448177a5283d24b
Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/i2-batch-i/I2-052-I-P01/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:hasCalculationCase ex:calculation .

ex:calculation a nltl:calculationCase ;
    nltl:hasInterpolationPoint ex:lowerPoint,
        ex:upperPoint ;
    nltl:hullGirderLongitudinalPositionFromAft ex:x ;
    nltl:interpolatedLongitudinalDistributionFactor ex:interpFactor ;
    nltl:interpolatedValue "0.5"^^xsd:string ;
    nltl:interpolationLowerEndpoint ex:lowerPoint ;
    nltl:interpolationUpperEndpoint ex:upperPoint .

ex:interpFactor a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 0.5 ;
    qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .

ex:lowerCoord a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 60.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M .

ex:lowerValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 0.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .

ex:upperCoord a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 90.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M .

ex:upperValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 1.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .

ex:x a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 75.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M .

ex:lowerPoint a nltl:interpolationPoint ;
    nltl:interpolationCoordinate ex:lowerCoord ;
    nltl:interpolationPointValue ex:lowerValue .

ex:upperPoint a nltl:interpolationPoint ;
    nltl:interpolationCoordinate ex:upperCoord ;
    nltl:interpolationPointValue ex:upperValue .


```

```turtle
ex:ship a nltl:ship ;
    nltl:hasCalculationCase ex:calculation .

ex:calculation a nltl:calculationCase ;
    nltl:hasInterpolationPoint ex:lowerPoint,
        ex:upperPoint ;
    nltl:hullGirderLongitudinalPositionFromAft ex:x ;
    nltl:interpolatedValue "0.5"^^xsd:string ;
    nltl:interpolationLowerEndpoint ex:lowerPoint ;
    nltl:interpolationUpperEndpoint ex:upperPoint .

ex:lowerCoord a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 60.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M .

ex:lowerValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 0.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .

ex:upperCoord a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 90.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M .

ex:upperValue a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 1.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:UNITLESS .

ex:x a qudt:QuantityValue ;
    qudt:numericValue 75.0 ;
    qudt:unit unit:M .

ex:lowerPoint a nltl:interpolationPoint ;
    nltl:interpolationCoordinate ex:lowerCoord ;
    nltl:interpolationPointValue ex:lowerValue .

ex:upperPoint a nltl:interpolationPoint ;
    nltl:interpolationCoordinate ex:upperCoord ;
    nltl:interpolationPointValue ex:upperValue .
```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:QuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A quantity value must provide a decimal numeric value."
    ] .

gen:LengthQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The longitudinal position must provide a decimal numeric value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The longitudinal position must use metres."
    ] .

gen:UnitlessQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The distribution factor must provide a decimal numeric value."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The distribution factor must be dimensionless."
    ] .

gen:InterpolationPointShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:interpolationPoint ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:interpolationCoordinate ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:QuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each interpolation point must provide an interpolation coordinate."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:interpolationPointValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:QuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each interpolation point must provide an interpolation point value."
    ] .

gen:CalculationCaseShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:calculationCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasInterpolationPoint ;
        sh:class nltl:interpolationPoint ;
        sh:node gen:InterpolationPointShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each calculation case must contain interpolation points."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:interpolationLowerEndpoint ;
        sh:class nltl:interpolationPoint ;
        sh:node gen:InterpolationPointShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each calculation case must identify a lower interpolation endpoint."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:interpolationUpperEndpoint ;
        sh:class nltl:interpolationPoint ;
        sh:node gen:InterpolationPointShape ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each calculation case must identify an upper interpolation endpoint."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hullGirderLongitudinalPositionFromAft ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:LengthQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each calculation case must provide the hull girder longitudinal position from aft."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:interpolatedLongitudinalDistributionFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:UnitlessQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each calculation case must provide the externally determined interpolated longitudinal distribution factor."
    ] .

gen:I2052ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasCalculationCase ;
        sh:class nltl:calculationCase ;

        sh:minCount 1 ;

    sh:property [

        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide the externally determined interpolated value."
    ] .
```

F. MANUAL REVIEW CHECKLIST — DO NOT PREFILL GRADES
Q1 Applicability: Does the shape activate for the correct ships, conditions, and scope?
Q2 Ownership / paths: Are target ownership and property paths correct?
Q3 Required constraints: Are all mandatory obligations represented?
Q4 Units / calculation: Are quantities, units, formulae, and comparisons correct?
Q5 Branches / boundaries: Are alternatives, exceptions, equality boundaries, and conditionals correct?
Q6 No extra restrictions: Does the shape avoid requirements not supported by the source?
Q7 Traceability / clarity: Is the implementation understandable and traceable to the cited evidence?
Record grades, reviewer identity, evidence, and notes in the workbook. Do not infer full-requirement correctness from these two cases.
